provider: providerUsed });\n\n      try {\n        llmResponse = await callWithRetry(providerUsed, payload, requestId, fallbackRetries, modelUsed);\n      } catch (fallbackError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_fail', {\n          reason: 'llm_fallback_failed',\n          provider: providerUsed,\n          error: fallbackError instanceof Error ? fallbackError.message : String(fallbackError),\n        });\n        return errorResponse(500, 'Post-processing failed', fallbackError instanceof Error ? fallbackError.message : String(fallbackError), { requestId });\n      }\n    } else {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_fail', {\n        reason: 'llm_failed_no_fallback',\n        provider,\n        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n      });\n      return errorResponse(500, 'Post-processing failed', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), { requestId });\n    }\n  }\n\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_attempt_done', {\n    provider: providerUsed,\n    outputChars: rawResponseLength(llmResponse.raw),\n    costUsd: llmResponse.costUsd,\n  });\n\n  let correctedText: string;\n  let costUsd = llmResponse.costUsd;\n\n  try {\n    const evaluation = evaluateCompletionResponse(llmResponse.raw, text);\n\n    if (evaluation.accepted) {\n      correctedText = evaluation.text;\n    } else if (evaluation.failure === 'prompt_leakage') {\n      // Recomputed only for the outputChars metric below — evaluateCompletionResponse\n      // already extracted this successfully, so this call cannot throw.\n      const leakedText = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(llmResponse.raw));\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.prompt_leakage_detected', {\n        provider: providerUsed,\n        inputChars: text.length,\n        outputChars: leakedText.length,\n      });\n\n      const alternateProvider: LLMProvider = fallbackProviderFor(providerUsed);\n      const alternateRetries = retriesFor(alternateProvider);\n\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_leakage_retry_start', { requestedProvider: providerUsed, provider: alternateProvider });\n\n      try {\n        const alternateModel = defaultModelFor(alternateProvider);\n        const retryResponse = await callWithRetry(alternateProvider, payload, requestId, alternateRetries, alternateModel);\n\n        // Bill the retry the moment it succeeds, before any evaluation step\n        // below can throw — otherwise a successful (and billed-by-the-provider)\n        // retry's cost would be silently dropped if extraction failed.\n        providerUsed = alternateProvider;\n        modelUsed = alternateModel;\n        costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd;\n\n        const retryEvaluation = evaluateCompletionResponse(retryResponse.raw, text);\n\n        if (retryEvaluation.accepted) {\n          correctedText = retryEvaluation.text;\n        } else if (retryEvaluation.failure === 'prompt_leakage') {\n          logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.prompt_leakage_persisted', {\n            provider: alternateProvider,\n            fallbackToRaw: true,\n          });\n          correctedText = text;\n        } else {\n          logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n            provider: alternateProvider,\n            state: retryEvaluation.state,\n            reason: retryEvaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ? 'empty_cleaned_text' : retryEvaluation.reason,\n            fallbackToRaw: true,\n          });\n          correctedText = text;\n        }\n      } catch (retryError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_leakage_retry_fail', {\n          provider: alternateProvider,\n          error: retryError instanceof Error ? retryError.message : String(retryError),\n          fallbackToRaw: true,\n        });\n        correctedText = text;\n      }\n    } else {\n      // output_limit, incomplete_response, or empty_cleaned_text: keep the\n      // raw transcript without spending on an alternate-provider retry.\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n        provider: providerUsed,\n        state: evaluation.state,\n        reason: evaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ? network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0ca973dd-8dba-46e2-91b3-afac1495e267","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"fdc3087c-f1f5-40e4-ab70-ff397b569461","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a463a803ef6c17681","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WTLdytDTUnQr1wVmrjt9mM","type":"tool_result","content":"// Anthropic's Messages API requires an explicit output ceiling. Providers with\n// optional limit fields omit them and use their model/API defaults instead.\nexport const ANTHROPIC_MAX_TOKENS = 8192;\n\n=== groq-llm ===\n// GROQ LLM CLIENT (CHAT COMPLETIONS)\n\nimport { computeGroqChatCost, estimateUsageFromChars, isGroqUsage, type GroqUsage } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { isRecord, safeReadText } from '../lib/utils';\n\nconst GROQ_BASE_URL = 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1';\nconst GROQ_CHAT_MODEL = 'openai/gpt-oss-120b';\n\nexport type ChatMessage = {\n  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool';\n  content: string;\n};\n\nexport type CorrectionRequestPayload = {\n  messages: ChatMessage[];\n  temperature: number;\n};\n\nexport async function requestGroqChat(\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  requestId: string\n): Promise<{ raw: unknown; usage?: GroqUsage; costUsd: number }> {\n  const apiKey = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    throw new Error('GROQ_API_KEY not configured');\n  }\n\n  const chatUrl = `${GROQ_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`;\n\n  const response = await fetch(chatUrl, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      'content-type': 'application/json',\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: GROQ_CHAT_MODEL,\n      ...payload,\n      reasoning_effort: 'low',\n      stream: false,\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const errorText = await safeReadText(response);\n    console.error('Groq LLM API returned error', {\n      requestId,\n      status: response.status,\n      statusText: response.statusText,\n      errorText,\n    });\n    const error = new Error(`Groq chat failed with status ${response.status}`);\n    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = response.status;\n    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'groq';\n    throw error;\n  }\n\n  const json = await response.json();\n  const usage = isRecord(json) && isGroqUsage(json['usage']) ? (json['usage'] as GroqUsage) : undefined;\n  const costUsd = computeGroqChatCost(usage ?? reportMissingUsage('groq', payload, json, requestId));\n\n  return {\n    raw: json,\n    usage,\n    costUsd,\n  };\n}\n\n// Fail-closed fallback for vendor usage-schema drift: estimate tokens from\n// character counts so a missing/unrecognized `usage` block is billed instead\n// of silently costing 0.\nexport function reportMissingUsage(\n  provider: string,\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  json: unknown,\n  requestId: string\n): GroqUsage {\n  const promptChars = payload.messages.reduce((sum, message) => sum + message.content.length, 0);\n\n  let completionChars = 0;\n  if (isRecord(json)) {\n    const choices = json['choices'];\n    const message = Array.isArray(choices) && isRecord(choices[0]) ? choices[0]['message'] : undefined;\n    const content = isRecord(message) ? message['content'] : undefined;\n    completionChars = typeof content === 'string' ? content.length : JSON.stringify(json).length;\n  }\n\n  const estimatedUsage = estimateUsageFromChars(promptChars, completionChars);\n  console.warn('LLM response missing/unrecognized usage; billing char-based estimate', {\n    requestId,\n    provider,\n    estimatedUsage,\n  });\n\n  return estimatedUsage;\n}\n\nexport function buildCorrectionRequest(systemPrompt: string, userContent: string): CorrectionRequestPayload {\n  return {\n    messages: [\n      { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },\n      { role: 'user', content: userContent },\n    ],\n    temperature: 0,\n  };\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8185e196-838d-48ef-a96a-ac84fef38fe5","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:22.796Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"// Anthropic's Messages API requires an explicit output ceiling. Providers with\n// optional limit fields omit them and use their model/API defaults instead.\nexport const ANTHROPIC_MAX_TOKENS = 8192;\n\n=== groq-llm ===\n// GROQ LLM CLIENT (CHAT COMPLETIONS)\n\nimport { computeGroqChatCost, estimateUsageFromChars, isGroqUsage, type GroqUsage } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { isRecord, safeReadText } from '../lib/utils';\n\nconst GROQ_BASE_URL = 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1';\nconst GROQ_CHAT_MODEL = 'openai/gpt-oss-120b';\n\nexport type ChatMessage = {\n  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool';\n  content: string;\n};\n\nexport type CorrectionRequestPayload = {\n  messages: ChatMessage[];\n  temperature: number;\n};\n\nexport async function requestGroqChat(\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  requestId: string\n): Promise<{ raw: unknown; usage?: GroqUsage; costUsd: number }> {\n  const apiKey = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    throw new Error('GROQ_API_KEY not configured');\n  }\n\n  const chatUrl = `${GROQ_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`;\n\n  const response = await fetch(chatUrl, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      'content-type': 'application/json',\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: GROQ_CHAT_MODEL,\n      ...payload,\n      reasoning_effort: 'low',\n      stream: false,\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const errorText = await safeReadText(response);\n    console.error('Groq LLM API returned error', {\n      requestId,\n      status: response.status,\n      statusText: response.statusText,\n      errorText,\n    });\n    const error = new Error(`Groq chat failed with status ${response.status}`);\n    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = response.status;\n    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'groq';\n    throw error;\n  }\n\n  const json = await response.json();\n  const usage = isRecord(json) && isGroqUsage(json['usage']) ? (json['usage'] as GroqUsage) : undefined;\n  const costUsd = computeGroqChatCost(usage ?? reportMissingUsage('groq', payload, json, requestId));\n\n  return {\n    raw: json,\n    usage,\n    costUsd,\n  };\n}\n\n// Fail-closed fallback for vendor usage-schema drift: estimate tokens from\n// character counts so a missing/unrecognized `usage` block is billed instead\n// of silently costing 0.\nexport function reportMissingUsage(\n  provider: string,\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  json: unknown,\n  requestId: string\n): GroqUsage {\n  const promptChars = payload.messages.reduce((sum, message) => sum + message.content.length, 0);\n\n  let completionChars = 0;\n  if (isRecord(json)) {\n    const choices = json['choices'];\n    const message = Array.isArray(choices) && isRecord(choices[0]) ? choices[0]['message'] : undefined;\n    const content = isRecord(message) ? message['content'] : undefined;\n    completionChars = typeof content === 'string' ? content.length : JSON.stringify(json).length;\n  }\n\n  const estimatedUsage = estimateUsageFromChars(promptChars, completionChars);\n  console.warn('LLM response missing/unrecognized usage; billing char-based estimate', {\n    requestId,\n    provider,\n    estimatedUsage,\n  });\n\n  return estimatedUsage;\n}\n\nexport function buildCorrectionRequest(systemPrompt: string, userContent: string): CorrectionRequestPayload {\n  return {\n    messages: [\n      { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },\n      { role: 'user', content: userContent },\n    ],\n    temperature: 0,\n  };\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fdc3087c-f1f5-40e4-ab70-ff397b569461","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The ≈ $0.10/hr async equivalence covers input-AUDIO +\n101\t// OUTPUT-text tokens for typical speech; custom-context INPUT-text tokens are\n102\t// charged ON TOP at $3.50/1M (async). Soniox bills ~0.3 tokens per character.\n103\t// Ref: https://soniox.com/pricing\n104\tconst SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.10 / 60;                       // ≈ $0.001667/min\n105\tconst SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 3.50 / 1_000_000;            // async context tokens\n106\tconst SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR = 0.3;\n107\texport function estimateSonioxContextTokens(contextText: string | undefined): number {\n108\t  if (!contextText) return 0;\n109\t  return Math.ceil(contextText.length * SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR);\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\t// Anthropic Claude Haiku 4.5 Pricing (USD)\n113\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.00 / 1_000_000;\n114\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n115\t// Prompt caching: writes bill at 1.25x input, reads at 0.10x input (5-minute TTL).\n116\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 1.25;\n117\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 0.10;\n118\t\n119\t// Cerebras GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\n120\tconst CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.35 / 1_000_000;\n121\tconst CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.75 / 1_000_000;\n122\t\n123\t// Groq GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\n124\tconst GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.15 / 1_000_000;\n125\tconst GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.60 / 1_000_000;\n126\t\n127\t// xAI Grok 4.1 Fast Pricing (USD)\n128\tconst XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\n129\tconst XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\n130\t\n131\t// ── HyperWhisper Cloud post-processing LLM providers (per-model) ────────────\n132\t// Each provider exposes one or more chat models with distinct input/output\n133\t// token pricing. These MUST match shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json\n134\t// (the display source of truth) — they are the ACTUAL billing constants. Rates\n135\t// are USD per-1M ÷ 1e6. Re-confirmed against live provider pricing 2026-06-19.\n136\tinterface LLMChatRate {\n137\t  promptPerToken: number;\n138\t  completionPerToken: number;\n139\t}\n140\t\n141\t// OpenAI GPT-5 family (chat/completions). https://platform.openai.com/docs/pricing\n142\tconst OPENAI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n143\t  'gpt-5-mini': { promptPerToken: 0.25 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.00 / 1_000_000 },\n144\t  'gpt-5-nano': { promptPerToken: 0.05 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n145\t};\n146\tconst OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gpt-5-mini';\n147\t\n148\t// Google Gemini (OpenAI-compatible endpoint). https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\n149\tconst GEMINI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n150\t  'gemini-2.5-flash': { promptPerToken: 0.30 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.50 / 1_000_000 },\n151\t  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { promptPerToken: 0.10 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n152\t};\n153\tconst GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gemini-2.5-flash';\n154\t\n155\t// Mistral (chat/completions). https://mistral.ai/pricing + docs.mistral.ai\n156\t// `mistral-small-latest` resolves to Mistral Small 4: the model-specific docs\n157\t// list $0.15/$0.60, which conflicts with the lower pricing-tile figure — we\n158\t// bill at the higher (model-docs) rate, the billing-safe choice. `mistral-nemo`\n159\t// is not a valid chat id; the canonical API id is `open-mistral-nemo`.\n160\t// Re-confirm both against live provider pages before secrets go live.\n161\tconst MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n162\t  'mistral-small-latest': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.60 / 1_000_000 },\n163\t  'open-mistral-nemo': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000 },\n164\t};\n165\tconst MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'mistral-small-latest';\n166\t\n167\t// Credit model: 1 credit = $0.001\n168\tconst USD_PER_CREDIT = 0.001;\n169\t\n170\t// =============================================================================\n171\t// TYPES\n172\t// =============================================================================\n173\t\n174\texport interface GroqUsage {\n175\t  prompt_tokens: number;\n176\t  completion_tokens: number;\n177\t  total_tokens: number;\n178\t}\n179\t"}]},"uuid":"8716494b-a701-4aec-9067-b9e2a7820982","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:39.206Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/cost-calculator.ts","content":"// Soniox — token-billed. The ≈ $0.10/hr async equivalence covers input-AUDIO +\n// OUTPUT-text tokens for typical speech; custom-context INPUT-text tokens are\n// charged ON TOP at $3.50/1M (async). Soniox bills ~0.3 tokens per character.\n// Ref: https://soniox.com/pricing\nconst SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.10 / 60;                       // ≈ $0.001667/min\nconst SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 3.50 / 1_000_000;            // async context tokens\nconst SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR = 0.3;\nexport function estimateSonioxContextTokens(contextText: string | undefined): number {\n  if (!contextText) return 0;\n  return Math.ceil(contextText.length * SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR);\n}\n\n// Anthropic Claude Haiku 4.5 Pricing (USD)\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.00 / 1_000_000;\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n// Prompt caching: writes bill at 1.25x input, reads at 0.10x input (5-minute TTL).\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 1.25;\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 0.10;\n\n// Cerebras GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\nconst CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.35 / 1_000_000;\nconst CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.75 / 1_000_000;\n\n// Groq GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\nconst GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.15 / 1_000_000;\nconst GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.60 / 1_000_000;\n\n// xAI Grok 4.1 Fast Pricing (USD)\nconst XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\nconst XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\n\n// ── HyperWhisper Cloud post-processing LLM providers (per-model) ────────────\n// Each provider exposes one or more chat models with distinct input/output\n// token pricing. These MUST match shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json\n// (the display source of truth) — they are the ACTUAL billing constants. Rates\n// are USD per-1M ÷ 1e6. Re-confirmed against live provider pricing 2026-06-19.\ninterface LLMChatRate {\n  promptPerToken: number;\n  completionPerToken: number;\n}\n\n// OpenAI GPT-5 family (chat/completions). https://platform.openai.com/docs/pricing\nconst OPENAI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'gpt-5-mini': { promptPerToken: 0.25 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.00 / 1_000_000 },\n  'gpt-5-nano': { promptPerToken: 0.05 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gpt-5-mini';\n\n// Google Gemini (OpenAI-compatible endpoint). https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\nconst GEMINI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'gemini-2.5-flash': { promptPerToken: 0.30 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.50 / 1_000_000 },\n  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { promptPerToken: 0.10 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gemini-2.5-flash';\n\n// Mistral (chat/completions). https://mistral.ai/pricing + docs.mistral.ai\n// `mistral-small-latest` resolves to Mistral Small 4: the model-specific docs\n// list $0.15/$0.60, which conflicts with the lower pricing-tile figure — we\n// bill at the higher (model-docs) rate, the billing-safe choice. `mistral-nemo`\n// is not a valid chat id; the canonical API id is `open-mistral-nemo`.\n// Re-confirm both against live provider pages before secrets go live.\nconst MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'mistral-small-latest': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.60 / 1_000_000 },\n  'open-mistral-nemo': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'mistral-small-latest';\n\n// Credit model: 1 credit = $0.001\nconst USD_PER_CREDIT = 0.001;\n\n// =============================================================================\n// TYPES\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport interface GroqUsage {\n  prompt_tokens: number;\n  completion_tokens: number;\n  total_tokens: number;\n}\n","numLines":80,"startLine":100,"totalLines":521}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"44c6750a-8691-48f8-bd43-090b75ed0cf6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"2dbf9ebd-e7c6-4836-9372-d338c54b0994","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a463a803ef6c17681","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0188cxxk9fjpcWxuiYKg3m4b","type":"tool_result","content":"import { isRecord } from './utils';\nimport { containsPromptLeakage, extractCorrectedText, stripCleanMarkers } from './text-processing';\n\nexport type LLMCompletionStatus =\n  | { state: 'complete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'output_limit'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'incomplete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'unspecified'; reason: string };\n\n/**\n * Normalize terminal reasons from Anthropic Messages and OpenAI-compatible\n * Chat Completions responses. Recognized terminal reasons are classified as\n * complete, output-limited, or incomplete, and only a recognized\n * non-terminal reason (e.g. `length`, `content_filter`, `refusal`) fails\n * closed. Missing termination metadata — no response object, no stop_reason,\n * no finish_reason — is `unspecified` rather than a rejection: some\n * custom/self-hosted servers omit it entirely, and callers proceed to text\n * handling for `unspecified` the same as they do for `complete`.\n */\nexport function getLLMCompletionStatus(raw: unknown): LLMCompletionStatus {\n  if (!isRecord(raw)) {\n    return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_response_object' };\n  }\n\n  const anthropicReason = raw['stop_reason'];\n  if (typeof anthropicReason === 'string') {\n    if (anthropicReason === 'end_turn' || anthropicReason === 'stop_sequence') {\n      return { state: 'complete', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    if (anthropicReason === 'max_tokens') {\n      return { state: 'output_limit', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: anthropicReason };\n  }\n\n  const choices = raw['choices'];\n  const firstChoice = Array.isArray(choices) ? choices[0] : undefined;\n  const finishReason = isRecord(firstChoice) ? firstChoice['finish_reason'] : undefined;\n\n  if (finishReason === 'stop') {\n    return { state: 'complete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (finishReason === 'length') {\n    return { state: 'output_limit', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (typeof finishReason === 'string') {\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n\n  return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_finish_reason' };\n}\n\nexport type CompletionFailure = 'none' | 'output_limit' | 'incomplete_response' | 'prompt_leakage' | 'empty_cleaned_text';\n\n/**\n * Composed completion policy — the TS analog of the Rust core's\n * `evaluate_completion`. Gates on completion state, extracts and strips\n * markers, then rejects on prompt leakage or an empty result. A throw from\n * `extractCorrectedText` (a response with no text field anywhere) is\n * genuinely malformed and propagates to the caller rather than being\n * absorbed here.\n */\nexport function evaluateCompletionResponse(\n  raw: unknown,\n  original: string\n): { accepted: boolean; text: string; failure: CompletionFailure; state: LLMCompletionStatus['state']; reason: string } {\n  const status = getLLMCompletionStatus(raw);\n\n  if (status.state !== 'complete' && status.state !== 'unspecified') {\n    const failure: CompletionFailure = status.state === 'output_limit' ? 'output_limit' : 'incomplete_response';\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure, state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  const cleaned = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(raw));\n\n  if (containsPromptLeakage(cleaned)) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'prompt_leakage', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  if (cleaned.length === 0) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'empty_cleaned_text', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  return { accepted: true, text: cleaned, failure: 'none', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e4fe54ac-c423-4e41-bcd0-f3484a9883bb","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:58.788Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import { isRecord } from './utils';\nimport { containsPromptLeakage, extractCorrectedText, stripCleanMarkers } from './text-processing';\n\nexport type LLMCompletionStatus =\n  | { state: 'complete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'output_limit'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'incomplete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'unspecified'; reason: string };\n\n/**\n * Normalize terminal reasons from Anthropic Messages and OpenAI-compatible\n * Chat Completions responses. Recognized terminal reasons are classified as\n * complete, output-limited, or incomplete, and only a recognized\n * non-terminal reason (e.g. `length`, `content_filter`, `refusal`) fails\n * closed. Missing termination metadata — no response object, no stop_reason,\n * no finish_reason — is `unspecified` rather than a rejection: some\n * custom/self-hosted servers omit it entirely, and callers proceed to text\n * handling for `unspecified` the same as they do for `complete`.\n */\nexport function getLLMCompletionStatus(raw: unknown): LLMCompletionStatus {\n  if (!isRecord(raw)) {\n    return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_response_object' };\n  }\n\n  const anthropicReason = raw['stop_reason'];\n  if (typeof anthropicReason === 'string') {\n    if (anthropicReason === 'end_turn' || anthropicReason === 'stop_sequence') {\n      return { state: 'complete', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    if (anthropicReason === 'max_tokens') {\n      return { state: 'output_limit', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: anthropicReason };\n  }\n\n  const choices = raw['choices'];\n  const firstChoice = Array.isArray(choices) ? choices[0] : undefined;\n  const finishReason = isRecord(firstChoice) ? firstChoice['finish_reason'] : undefined;\n\n  if (finishReason === 'stop') {\n    return { state: 'complete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (finishReason === 'length') {\n    return { state: 'output_limit', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (typeof finishReason === 'string') {\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n\n  return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_finish_reason' };\n}\n\nexport type CompletionFailure = 'none' | 'output_limit' | 'incomplete_response' | 'prompt_leakage' | 'empty_cleaned_text';\n\n/**\n * Composed completion policy — the TS analog of the Rust core's\n * `evaluate_completion`. Gates on completion state, extracts and strips\n * markers, then rejects on prompt leakage or an empty result. A throw from\n * `extractCorrectedText` (a response with no text field anywhere) is\n * genuinely malformed and propagates to the caller rather than being\n * absorbed here.\n */\nexport function evaluateCompletionResponse(\n  raw: unknown,\n  original: string\n): { accepted: boolean; text: string; failure: CompletionFailure; state: LLMCompletionStatus['state']; reason: string } {\n  const status = getLLMCompletionStatus(raw);\n\n  if (status.state !== 'complete' && status.state !== 'unspecified') {\n    const failure: CompletionFailure = status.state === 'output_limit' ? 'output_limit' : 'incomplete_response';\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure, state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  const cleaned = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(raw));\n\n  if (containsPromptLeakage(cleaned)) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'prompt_leakage', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  if (cleaned.length === 0) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'empty_cleaned_text', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  return { accepted: true, text: cleaned, failure: 'none', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2dbf9ebd-e7c6-4836-9372-d338c54b0994","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Without these in allowHeaders the preflight blocks the\n// POST before it reaches the route (X-STT-* are non-simple request headers).\napp.use('*', cors({\n  origin: '*',\n  allowHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'X-STT-Provider', 'X-STT-Model', 'X-STT-Domain'],\n  allowMethods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS'],\n}));\n\napp.options('*', (c) => c.body(null, 204));\n\n// Health check endpoint - Fly.io uses this for health monitoring\napp.get('/health', (c) => {\n  return c.json({\n    status: 'ok',\n    region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n});\n\n// Warmup endpoint - clients hit this on hotkey-down to pre-establish\n// the TLS/HTTP2 connection before the /transcribe POST.\napp.get('/warmup', (c) => {\n  c.header('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n  return c.body(null, 204);\n});\n\n// Main transcription endpoint\napp.post('/transcribe', transcribeRoute);\n\n// Standalone post-processing endpoint\napp.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute);\n\n// Assistant mode endpoint (vision LLM)\napp.post('/assistant', assistantRoute);\n\n// Usage endpoint\napp.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n\n// WebSocket streaming endpoint\napp.get('/ws/streaming-deepgram', wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute);\n\n// Fallback - match Cloudflare (405, plain text)\napp.notFound((c) => {\n  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n});\n\n// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Log the full error\n// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\napp.onError((err, c) => {\n  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n  return c.json({\n    error: 'Internal server error',\n    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. This replaces the older\n      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n      ORDER BY\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n    let deductedAmount = 0;\n\n    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n\n      const grant = result.rows[index];\n      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n\n      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n      deductedAmount += deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n    // transaction (and same locked view). The public API response shape is\n * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n */\nexport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8ada99f7-a78a-44e7-86dd-9c00de613253","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

missing metadata proceeds so custom OpenAI-compatible endpoints keep working.\n- Lenient marker handling with a distinguishable failure taxonomy; prompt leakage is rejected on both the wrapped and markerless paths.\n- Cloud: shared evaluateCompletionResponse helper; successful alternate-provider retries are always billed; present-but-empty content falls back to the raw transcript instead of a billed 500.\n- Windows: provider plumbing deduped, endpoint probe reuses the real request builder, prompt-budget guard runs before KV-cache allocation.\n- New shared-core-tests workflow runs cargo test + clippy on core/conformance changes.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0cccedbb-fee2-41ee-9acf-e70a8ff2aa68","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:51.084Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"3b810e6 feat: vendor Fly backend as hyperwhisper-cloud/, move deploy workflows to app repo\n---show 3b810e6a msg---\nfeat: vendor Fly backend as hyperwhisper-cloud/, move deploy workflows to app repo\n\nConvert the backend-v2-flyio git submodule (github.com/ray-amjad/hyperwhisper-fly\n@ b474c6f) into a plain tracked folder hyperwhisper-cloud/. buildCorrectionRequest } from './groq-llm';\nimport { buildOpenAIBody } from './openai-llm';\n\ndescribe('hosted LLM output-limit policy', () => {\n  test('omits optional output caps from the shared provider payload', () => {\n    const payload = buildCorrectionRequest('system', 'transcript');\n\n    expect(payload).not.toHaveProperty('max_tokens');\n    expect(payload).not.toHaveProperty('max_completion_tokens');\n  });\n\n  test('does not translate an output cap into the OpenAI request', () => {\n    const body = buildOpenAIBody(buildCorrectionRequest('system', 'transcript'), 'gpt-5-mini');\n\n    expect(body).not.toHaveProperty('max_tokens');\n    expect(body).not.toHaveProperty('max_completion_tokens');\n  });\n\n  test('keeps Anthropic required max_tokens at 8192', () => {\n    expect(ANTHROPIC_MAX_TOKENS).toBe(8192);\n  });\n\n  test('keeps Anthropic aligned with the strict cleaned-wrapper contract', {"parentUuid":"e12113ed-77c1-4f66-9617-3ae13b9f325c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a463a803ef6c17681","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJF2d8yejwusWU74g7p","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## Verdicts\n\n---\n\n**`[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts:18]` CONFIRMED**\n\n**Trigger path:** `index.ts:48 app.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute)` → `postProcessRoute` → `validateCredits(authResult.value, ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS, clientIP)` (line 118) → `callWithRetry(provider, payload, …)` (line 140) → `deductCredits(authResult.value, costUsd, …)` (line 280).\n\n**Triggering input:** `POST /post-process`, `Content-Type: application/json`, `X-LLM-Provider: grok`, body `{\"account_key\":\"<key with a 1.0 credit balance>\",\"prompt\":\"Fix grammar.\",\"text\":\"<100,000 chars>\"}`.\n\nThe gate is a hard block, not advisory — `middleware/credits.ts:46-49`:\n```ts\nconst balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\nif (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n  return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n}\n```\n…but `estimatedCredits` is the flat `1.0` from line 18, while the deduction at line 278/280 is the provider's true token cost.\n\n**Quantified.** 1 credit = $0.001 (`cost-calculator.ts:168 const USD_PER_CREDIT = 0.001`). 100k chars ≈ 25k input tokens plus a similar-length corrected output (no output ceiling exists on any non-Anthropic provider — `llm-request-policy.test.ts:9-21` deliberately asserts `max_tokens` is *absent*).\n- default `cerebras` ($0.35/$0.75 per M, `cost-calculator.ts:120-121`) → ≈ $0.0275 → **≈28 credits vs 1.0 reserved**\n- `X-LLM-Provider: grok` ($1.25/$2.50 per M, lines 128-129) → ≈ $0.094 → **≈94 credits vs 1.0 reserved**\n\nThis is not only an adversarial case: on the default provider the break-even is ~3,600 chars of `text`, so **any ordinary long dictation already exceeds the reservation.**\n\n**Where the loss lands (verified, not assumed):** the balance does *not* go negative. `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:115-117` → `deductCreditBalance` → `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` (`nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:582-607`) drains grants in a loop and simply leaves `remainingToDeduct` unspent when they run out; the route comment reads *\"Atomic SQL decrement (floored at 0)\"*. So the overage is unrecoverable provider spend, not carryable debt.\n\n**One correction to the candidate's repeatability claim:** serially it is *not* \"repeatable indefinitely from a single near-zero-balance key\" — `recordLicenseUsage` writes `credits_remaining` back into the `license:<key>` cache (`credits.ts:85-91`), which `validateAuth` reads (`auth.ts:110-120`), so the next request sees 0 and is blocked. The real amplifier is **concurrency**: `deductCredits` is fire-and-forget after the response and `/post-process` has no rate limit (only `isIPBlocked`), so N simultaneous requests on a 1.0-credit key all clear the same gate → N × ~94 credits. That does make the aggregate unbounded.\n\n**Intent check — no evidence of deliberateness.** `git blame -L 17,18` → `3b810e6` (\"feat: vendor Fly backend as hyperwhisper-cloud/\"), a pure vendoring commit with no rationale; `git log -S ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS` returns only that commit; there is no `post-process.test.ts` anywhere in the repo and no test references the constant; no comment accompanies it. The opposite way: `transcribe.ts:125-128` documents the discipline this route violates — *\"The reservation uses the most expensive member of the chain so we never under-reserve.\"* I1 is a stated design goal that `/post-process` does not meet.\n\n---\n\n**`[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts:93]` CONFIRMED (mechanism) — magnitude in the failure scenario REFUTED**\n\nThe asymmetry is real and code-visible. `text` is capped:\n```ts\nif (text.length > MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {   // line 86\n```\n`prompt` is not:\n```ts\nconst prompt = typeof body.prompt === 'string' ? body.prompt.trim() : '';   // line 93\nif (!prompt) { … }                                                          // only an emptiness check\n```\nand it is forwarded verbatim as the system message (`buildCorrectionRequest(prompt, userContent)`, line 132 → `groq-llm.ts` `{ role: 'system', content: systemPrompt }`), billed as input tokens by every `compute*ChatCost`. I confirmed the body-limit claim: `index.ts` mounts the route bare and `export default { port, fetch, websocket }` sets no `maxRequestBodySize`, so only Bun's 128 MB default applies.\n\n**Working trigger:** `{\"text\":\"hi\",\"prompt\":\"<200 KB of text>\"}` on a 1.0-credit key → ~50k input tokens → cerebras $0.0175 ≈ **18 credits against a 1.0 reservation**.\n\n**But the stated 20 MB / ~5M-token / \"orders of magnitude\" scenario does not hold.** An over-context request returns 4xx from every provider, and `shouldFallback` (`llm-provider.ts:181-184`) only falls back on 5xx:\n```ts\nreturn typeof status === 'number' && status >= 500 && status <= 599;\n```\nso a 20 MB prompt costs **$0** and returns a 500 to the caller. The real ceiling is the model's context window, which puts this in the same tens-to-low-hundreds-of-credits band as the line-18 finding, not above it.\n\n**Relationship to line 18:** same root cause (the reservation is not a function of input size). Worth keeping as a separate item only because a fix that scales the reservation on `text.length` alone would leave `prompt` unreserved — it is not a second, independent order-of-magnitude loss. **Recommend deduplicating the magnitude when reporting.**\n\n**Intent check:** `git blame -L 93,96` → `3b810e6` (the vendoring commit), no comment, no test.\n\n---\n\n**`[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts:213]` REFUTED — intentional, and not an independent I1 violation**\n\n**Intentional:** the line carries its own explaining comment (208-210) —\n```ts\n// Bill the retry the moment it succeeds, before any evaluation step\n// below can throw — otherwise a successful (and billed-by-the-provider)\n// retry's cost would be silently dropped if extraction failed.\n```\n— and `git blame -L 199,215` points at `28de2ec6` (\"Unify LLM post-processing completion policy in shared-core-rs (#39)\"), whose message states the rule verbatim: *\"successful alternate-provider retries are always billed\"*. Summing at line 213 is the deliberate **fix** for I2; removing it would be the bug.\n\n**The claim misframes the design.** \"No second `validateCredits` call\" is not a missing guard: the reservation is a preflight *admission gate*, not an escrow. A check inserted at line 213 would run after call 1's money is already spent, and would read `auth.credits` — a value captured before the request and never refreshed mid-flight (`auth.ts:110-141`) — so it could not prevent any spend.\n\n**Not independently a violation.** The leakage retry is at most a 2× factor, so it can only breach the 1.0 reservation when a *single* call already costs ≥0.5 credits — i.e. only when line 18 is already under-reserving. It amplifies the line-18 finding; it does not create one.\n\n**One genuinely reportable sub-finding, though it is quality/latency, not I1:** the retry is deterministically user-triggerable. `buildTranscriptUserContent` wraps input in literal markers (`text-processing.ts:157-159`):\n```ts\nreturn `--TRANSCRIPT--\\n${text}\\n--ENDTRANSCRIPT--`;\n```\nand `containsPromptLeakage` (176-177) tests the model's *output* for those same strings. So any `text` containing the literal `--TRANSCRIPT--` makes a faithful correction look like leakage, forcing a second provider call and then returning the raw transcript unchanged (line 224). Nothing sanitizes `text` for these markers. Real, but it burns latency and a second completion — it only breaks I1 via line 18."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2538,"cache_read_input_tokens":92737,"output_tokens":3021,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2538},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3021,"cache_read_input_tokens":92737,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2538,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2538,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWJF1mnqsYTQZXCv44UJ","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"cc53e1be-38fe-4fbd-9ca8-dd1bec521bad","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:50:27.617Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
